## Instruction Priority

Superpowers skills override default system prompt behavior, but **user instructions always take precedence**:

1. **User's explicit instructions** (CLAUDE.md, GEMINI.md, AGENTS.md, direct requests) — highest priority
2. **Superpowers skills** — override default system behavior where they conflict
3. **Default system prompt** — lowest priority

If CLAUDE.md, GEMINI.md, or AGENTS.md says "don't use TDD" and a skill says "always use TDD," follow the user's instructions. The user is in control.

## How to Access Skills

**In Claude Code:** Use the `Skill` tool. When you invoke a skill, its content is loaded and presented to you—follow it directly. Never use the Read tool on skill files.

**In Copilot CLI:** Use the `skill` tool. Skills are auto-discovered from installed plugins. The `skill` tool works the same as Claude Code's `Skill` tool.

**In Gemini CLI:** Skills activate via the `activate_skill` tool. Gemini loads skill metadata at session start and activates the full content on demand.

**In other environments:** Check your platform's documentation for how skills are loaded.

## Platform Adaptation

Skills use Claude Code tool names. Non-CC platforms: see `references/copilot-tools.md` (Copilot CLI), `references/codex-tools.md` (Codex) for tool equivalents. Gemini CLI users get the tool mapping loaded automatically via GEMINI.md.

# Using Skills

## The Rule

**Invoke relevant or requested skills BEFORE any response or action.** Even a 1% chance a skill might apply means that you should invoke the skill to check. If an invoked skill turns out to be wrong for the situation, you don't need to use it.

```dot
digraph skill_flow {
    "User message received" [shape=doublecircle];
    "About to EnterPlanMode?" [shape=doublecircle];
    "Already brainstormed?" [shape=diamond];
    "Invoke brainstorming skill" [shape=box];
    "Might any skill apply?" [shape=diamond];
    "Invoke Skill tool" [shape=box];
    "Announce: 'Using [skill] to [purpose]'" [shape=box];
    "Has checklist?" [shape=diamond];
    "Create TodoWrite todo per item" [shape=box];
    "Follow skill exactly" [shape=box];
    "Respond (including clarifications)" [shape=doublecircle];

    "About to EnterPlanMode?" -> "Already brainstormed?";
    "Already brainstormed?" -> "Invoke brainstorming skill" [label="no"];
    "Already brainstormed?" -> "Might any skill apply?" [label="yes"];
    "Invoke brainstorming skill" -> "Might any skill apply?";

    "User message received" -> "Might any skill apply?";
    "Might any skill apply?" -> "Invoke Skill tool" [label="yes, even 1%"];
    "Might any skill apply?" -> "Respond (including clarifications)" [label="definitely not"];
    "Invoke Skill tool" -> "Announce: 'Using [skill] to [purpose]'";
    "Announce: 'Using [skill] to [purpose]'" -> "Has checklist?";
    "Has checklist?" -> "Create TodoWrite todo per item" [label="yes"];
    "Has checklist?" -> "Follow skill exactly" [label="no"];
    "Create TodoWrite todo per item" -> "Follow skill exactly";
}
```

## Red Flags

These thoughts mean STOP—you're rationalizing:

| Thought | Reality |
|---------|---------|
| "This is just a simple question" | Questions are tasks. Check for skills. |
| "I need more context first" | Skill check comes BEFORE clarifying questions. |
| "Let me explore the codebase first" | Skills tell you HOW to explore. Check first. |
| "I can check git/files quickly" | Files lack conversation context. Check for skills. |
| "Let me gather information first" | Skills tell you HOW to gather information. |
| "This doesn't need a formal skill" | If a skill exists, use it. |
| "I remember this skill" | Skills evolve. Read current version. |
| "This doesn't count as a task" | Action = task. Check for skills. |
| "The skill is overkill" | Simple things become complex. Use it. |
| "I'll just do this one thing first" | Check BEFORE doing anything. |
| "This feels productive" | Undisciplined action wastes time. Skills prevent this. |
| "I know what that means" | Knowing the concept ≠ using the skill. Invoke it. |

## Skill Priority

When multiple skills could apply, use this order:

1. **Process skills first** (brainstorming, debugging) - these determine HOW to approach the task
2. **Implementation skills second** (frontend-design, mcp-builder) - these guide execution

"Let's build X" → brainstorming first, then implementation skills.
"Fix this bug" → debugging first, then domain-specific skills.

## Skill Types

**Rigid** (TDD, debugging): Follow exactly. Don't adapt away discipline.

**Flexible** (patterns): Adapt principles to context.

The skill itself tells you which.

## User Instructions

Instructions say WHAT, not HOW. "Add X" or "Fix Y" doesn't mean skip workflows.

name: verification-before-completion

# Verification Before Completion

## Overview

Claiming work is complete without verification is dishonesty, not efficiency.

**Core principle:** Evidence before claims, always.

**Violating the letter of this rule is violating the spirit of this rule.**

## The Iron Law

```
NO COMPLETION CLAIMS WITHOUT FRESH VERIFICATION EVIDENCE
```

If you haven't run the verification command in this message, you cannot claim it passes.

## The Gate Function

```
BEFORE claiming any status or expressing satisfaction:

1. IDENTIFY: What command proves this claim?
2. RUN: Execute the FULL command (fresh, complete)
3. READ: Full output, check exit code, count failures
4. VERIFY: Does output confirm the claim?
   - If NO: State actual status with evidence
   - If YES: State claim WITH evidence
5. ONLY THEN: Make the claim

Skip any step = lying, not verifying
```

## Common Failures

| Claim | Requires | Not Sufficient |
|-------|----------|----------------|
| Tests pass | Test command output: 0 failures | Previous run, "should pass" |
| Linter clean | Linter output: 0 errors | Partial check, extrapolation |
| Build succeeds | Build command: exit 0 | Linter passing, logs look good |
| Bug fixed | Test original symptom: passes | Code changed, assumed fixed |
| Regression test works | Red-green cycle verified | Test passes once |
| Agent completed | VCS diff shows changes | Agent reports "success" |
| Requirements met | Line-by-line checklist | Tests passing |

## Red Flags - STOP

- Using "should", "probably", "seems to"
- Expressing satisfaction before verification ("Great!", "Perfect!", "Done!", etc.)
- About to commit/push/PR without verification
- Trusting agent success reports
- Relying on partial verification
- Thinking "just this once"
- Tired and wanting work over
- **ANY wording implying success without having run verification**

## Rationalization Prevention

| Excuse | Reality |
|--------|---------|
| "Should work now" | RUN the verification |
| "I'm confident" | Confidence ≠ evidence |
| "Just this once" | No exceptions |
| "Linter passed" | Linter ≠ compiler |
| "Agent said success" | Verify independently |
| "I'm tired" | Exhaustion ≠ excuse |
| "Partial check is enough" | Partial proves nothing |
| "Different words so rule doesn't apply" | Spirit over letter |

## Key Patterns

**Tests:**
```
✅ [Run test command] [See: 34/34 pass] "All tests pass"
❌ "Should pass now" / "Looks correct"
```

**Regression tests (TDD Red-Green):**
```
✅ Write → Run (pass) → Revert fix → Run (MUST FAIL) → Restore → Run (pass)
❌ "I've written a regression test" (without red-green verification)
```

**Build:**
```
✅ [Run build] [See: exit 0] "Build passes"
❌ "Linter passed" (linter doesn't check compilation)
```

**Requirements:**
```
✅ Re-read plan → Create checklist → Verify each → Report gaps or completion
❌ "Tests pass, phase complete"
```

**Agent delegation:**
```
✅ Agent reports success → Check VCS diff → Verify changes → Report actual state
❌ Trust agent report
```

## Why This Matters

From 24 failure memories:
- your human partner said "I don't believe you" - trust broken
- Undefined functions shipped - would crash
- Missing requirements shipped - incomplete features
- Time wasted on false completion → redirect → rework
- Violates: "Honesty is a core value. If you lie, you'll be replaced."

## When To Apply

**ALWAYS before:**
- ANY variation of success/completion claims
- ANY expression of satisfaction
- ANY positive statement about work state
- Committing, PR creation, task completion
- Moving to next task
- Delegating to agents

**Rule applies to:**
- Exact phrases
- Paraphrases and synonyms
- Implications of success
- ANY communication suggesting completion/correctness

## The Bottom Line

**No shortcuts for verification.**

Run the command. Read the output. THEN claim the result.

This is non-negotiable.

# CLAUDE.md

> **GENERATED FROM agent-contract.md — DO NOT EDIT BY HAND.**
>
> Edit `agent-contract.md` then run `pnpm generate:configs`. CI fails if drift detected.

This file is the Claude Code entry point for the **SelfKnowledgeBaseWeb** monorepo. It tells
every Claude session what the project is, the hard rules, the agent roster, and the workflow.

- Spec: [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-04-29-self-knowledge-base-design.md](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-04-29-self-knowledge-base-design.md)
- Active plan: [docs/plans/active.md](docs/plans/active.md)
- Single source for agents: [agent-contract.md](agent-contract.md)
- Codex tool invocations: [docs/runbooks/codex-tool-invocations.md](docs/runbooks/codex-tool-invocations.md)

## Hard rules

1. **File size**: 200 lines (target) / 300 lines (ESLint `max-lines` warn) / 500 lines
   (`pnpm size-check` hard fail). Spec §3.6.
2. **Cross-file references**: every doc/code change must keep doc cross-references and
   contract files in sync (`packages/*/CONTRACT.md`, `agent-contract.md`).
3. **Lychee link-check**: broken markdown links block merge. Run `pnpm link-check` locally.
4. **`pnpm check` is mandatory before requesting review** (lint + typecheck + test + build + size).
5. **No direct git from workers**: only `git-operator` may commit / branch / rebase / push.
6. **No web access from workers**: only `researcher` may `web_search` / `web_fetch`.
7. **No cross-package moves from workers**: only `refactorer` may reorganize packages,
   and every reorganization requires an ADR under `docs/decisions/`.

## Commands cheat-sheet

| Command                 | Purpose                                               |
| ----------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- |
| `pnpm install`          | Install workspace dependencies                        |
| `pnpm dev`              | Run all `dev` scripts (parallel)                      |
| `pnpm check`            | Lint + typecheck + test + build + size-check          |
| `pnpm check:affected`   | Same, scoped to packages affected since `origin/main` |
| `pnpm size-check`       | Hard 500-line limit on every source file              |
| `pnpm link-check`       | Lychee scan over `./**/*.md`                          |
| `pnpm generate:configs` | Re-derive everything from `agent-contract.md`         |
| `pnpm format`           | Prettier write across the repo                        |

## Review workflow (spec §3.2 + ADR-0007 D2)

```
worker (writes code)
   │
   ▼
[Bash] codex exec --profile code-reviewer  (Codex 5.3-spark, line-level rigor, cheap default)
   │
   ├── if high-risk (D2 rows 1/2/4/8) → [Bash] codex exec --profile pr-gate  (Codex 5.5, deep scan)
   │
   ▼
pr-reviewer    (Claude, selective per ADR-0007 D2; spec match + regression + arch consistency)
   │
   ▼
git-operator   (Claude, only authorized git surface, runs `pnpm check` once more)
```

High-risk triggers per ADR-0007 D2 (4 rows +pr-gate +pr-reviewer, 4 rows +pr-reviewer only):
- **+pr-gate +pr-reviewer** (D2 rows 1/2/4/8): contract change, package add/remove, new ADR required, CI/deploy/auth/security touch.
- **+pr-reviewer only, skip pr-gate** (D2 rows 3/5/6/7): spec/agent-contract/ADR edit, delete/rename package, cross ≥3 packages, performance-auditor flagged.

See [ADR-0007 D2](docs/decisions/ADR-0007-job-function-codex-heavy-execution.md).

Codex tools (code-reviewer / pr-gate / plan-challenger / 5 scaffolders) are
[orchestrator-direct Bash invocations](docs/runbooks/codex-tool-invocations.md) post ADR-0007 D5,
not teammate spawns.

    21	}: EditorToolbarProps): ReactNode {
    22	  if (!editor) return null;
    23

    25	    <BubbleMenu editor={editor} className="skb-editor-toolbar">
    26	      <div role="toolbar" aria-label="Text formatting" className="skb-editor-toolbar__row">

    35	interface ToolbarButtonViewProps {
    36	  readonly button: ToolbarButton;
    37	  readonly editor: Editor;
    38	}
    39	
    40	function ToolbarButtonView({ button, editor }: ToolbarButtonViewProps): ReactNode {
    41	  const Icon = TOOLBAR_ICONS[button.icon];
    42	  const isActive = isDefaultCommandActive(editor, button.command);
    43	  const ariaLabel = button.shortcut
    44	    ? `${button.label} (${button.shortcut})`
    45	    : button.label;
    46	
    47	  return (
    48	    <button
    49	      type="button"
    50	      onClick={() => runDefaultCommand(button.command, editor.chain())}
    51	      aria-label={ariaLabel}
    52	      aria-pressed={isActive}
    53	      data-command={button.command}
    54	      className="skb-editor-toolbar__button"
    55	      data-active={isActive ? 'true' : 'false'}
    56	    >
    57	      {Icon ? <Icon /> : <span>{button.label[0]}</span>}
    58	    </button>
    59	  );
    60	}

     1	import type { ChainedCommands, Editor } from '@tiptap/core';
     2	
     3	interface ProseChainedCommands {
     4	  focus: () => ProseChainedCommands;
     5	  run: () => boolean;
     6	  toggleBold: () => ProseChainedCommands;
     7	  toggleItalic: () => ProseChainedCommands;
     8	  toggleStrike: () => ProseChainedCommands;
     9	  toggleCode: () => ProseChainedCommands;
    10	  toggleHeading: (attrs: { level: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 | 6 }) => ProseChainedCommands;
    11	  toggleBulletList: () => ProseChainedCommands;
    12	  toggleOrderedList: () => ProseChainedCommands;
    13	}
    14	
    15	export function runDefaultCommand(
    16	  name: string,
    17	  chain: ChainedCommands,
    18	): boolean {
    19	  const focused = (chain as unknown as ProseChainedCommands).focus();
    20	  switch (name) {
    21	    case 'toggleBold':
    22	      return focused.toggleBold().run();
    23	    case 'toggleItalic':
    24	      return focused.toggleItalic().run();
    25	    case 'toggleStrike':
    26	      return focused.toggleStrike().run();
    27	    case 'toggleCode':
    28	      return focused.toggleCode().run();
    29	    case 'toggleHeading1':
    30	      return focused.toggleHeading({ level: 1 }).run();
    31	    case 'toggleHeading2':
    32	      return focused.toggleHeading({ level: 2 }).run();
    33	    case 'toggleHeading3':
    34	      return focused.toggleHeading({ level: 3 }).run();
    35	    case 'toggleBulletList':
    36	      return focused.toggleBulletList().run();
    37	    case 'toggleOrderedList':
    38	      return focused.toggleOrderedList().run();
    39	    default:
    40	      return false;
    41	  }
    42	}
    43	
    44	export function isDefaultCommandActive(
    45	  editor: Editor,
    46	  command: string,
    47	): boolean {
    48	  switch (command) {
    49	    case 'toggleBold':
    50	      return editor.isActive('bold');
    51	    case 'toggleItalic':
    52	      return editor.isActive('italic');
    53	    case 'toggleStrike':
    54	      return editor.isActive('strike');
    55	    case 'toggleCode':
    56	      return editor.isActive('code');
    57	    case 'toggleHeading1':
    58	      return editor.isActive('heading', { level: 1 });
    59	    case 'toggleHeading2':
    60	      return editor.isActive('heading', { level: 2 });
    61	    case 'toggleHeading3':
    62	      return editor.isActive('heading', { level: 3 });
    63	    case 'toggleBulletList':
    64	      return editor.isActive('bulletList');
    65	    case 'toggleOrderedList':
    66	      return editor.isActive('orderedList');
    67	    default:
    68	      return false;
    69	  }
    70	}

    16	  })

     1	import { editBlockInputSchema } from '@skb/editor-commands';
     2	import { defaultToolbarConfig, type ToolbarButton, type ToolbarConfig } from './toolbar-config';
     3	
     4	export function findButton(
     5	  config: ToolbarConfig,
     6	  buttonId: string,
     7	): ToolbarButton | undefined {
     8	  return config.buttons.find((b) => b.id === buttonId);
     9	}
    10	
    11	export function listCommands(config: ToolbarConfig): readonly string[] {
    12	  return config.buttons.map((b) => b.command);
    13	}
    14	
    15	export function buildEditBlockInput(
    16	  pageSlug: string,
    17	  blockId: string,
    18	  draft: { props?: Record<string, unknown>; content?: string },
    19	): ReturnType<typeof editBlockInputSchema.parse> {
    20	  return editBlockInputSchema.parse({ pageSlug, blockId, ...draft });
    21	}
    22	
    23	export const defaultCommandBindings: Readonly<Record<string, string>> =
    24	  Object.freeze(
    25	    Object.fromEntries(defaultToolbarConfig.buttons.map((b) => [b.id, b.command])),
    26	  );

     1	import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
     2	import type { ChainedCommands, Editor } from '@tiptap/core';
     3	import { runDefaultCommand, isDefaultCommandActive } from '../ui-default/dispatch';
     4	import { defaultToolbarConfig } from '../core/toolbar-config';
     5	
     6	interface ChainCallLog {
     7	  readonly calls: string[];
     8	  readonly args: unknown[][];
     9	}
    10	
    11	function makeMockChain(): { chain: ChainedCommands; log: ChainCallLog } {
    12	  const log: ChainCallLog = { calls: [], args: [] };
    13	  const handler: ProxyHandler<object> = {
    14	    get(_target, prop) {
    15	      if (prop === 'run') return () => true;
    16	      const method = String(prop);
    17	      return (...args: unknown[]) => {
    18	        log.calls.push(method);
    19	        log.args.push(args);
    20	        return new Proxy({}, handler);
    21	      };
    22	    },
    23	  };
    24	  const chain = new Proxy({}, handler) as unknown as ChainedCommands;
    25	  return { chain, log };
    26	}
    27	
    28	describe('runDefaultCommand', () => {
    29	  it('routes toggleBold → chain.focus().toggleBold().run()', () => {
    30	    const { chain, log } = makeMockChain();
    31	    const result = runDefaultCommand('toggleBold', chain);
    32	    expect(result).toBe(true);
    33	    expect(log.calls).toEqual(['focus', 'toggleBold']);
    34	    expect(log.args[1]).toEqual([]);
    35	  });
    36	
    37	  it('routes toggleHeading1 → chain.focus().toggleHeading({level:1}).run() (NOT toggleHeading1)', () => {
    38	    const { chain, log } = makeMockChain();
    39	    runDefaultCommand('toggleHeading1', chain);
    40	    expect(log.calls).toEqual(['focus', 'toggleHeading']);
    41	    expect(log.args[1]).toEqual([{ level: 1 }]);
    42	  });
    43	
    44	  it('routes toggleHeading2 / toggleHeading3 with correct level args', () => {
    45	    const r2 = makeMockChain();
    46	    runDefaultCommand('toggleHeading2', r2.chain);
    47	    expect(r2.log.calls).toEqual(['focus', 'toggleHeading']);
    48	    expect(r2.log.args[1]).toEqual([{ level: 2 }]);
    49	
    50	    const r3 = makeMockChain();
    51	    runDefaultCommand('toggleHeading3', r3.chain);
    52	    expect(r3.log.calls).toEqual(['focus', 'toggleHeading']);
    53	    expect(r3.log.args[1]).toEqual([{ level: 3 }]);
    54	  });
    55	
    56	  it('routes toggleBulletList / toggleOrderedList correctly', () => {
    57	    const r1 = makeMockChain();
    58	    runDefaultCommand('toggleBulletList', r1.chain);
    59	    expect(r1.log.calls).toEqual(['focus', 'toggleBulletList']);
    60	
    61	    const r2 = makeMockChain();
    62	    runDefaultCommand('toggleOrderedList', r2.chain);
    63	    expect(r2.log.calls).toEqual(['focus', 'toggleOrderedList']);
    64	  });
    65	
    66	  it('routes toggleItalic / toggleStrike / toggleCode correctly', () => {
    67	    for (const cmd of ['toggleItalic', 'toggleStrike', 'toggleCode']) {
    68	      const { chain, log } = makeMockChain();
    69	      runDefaultCommand(cmd, chain);
    70	      expect(log.calls).toEqual(['focus', cmd]);
    71	    }
    72	  });
    73	
    74	  it('returns false for unknown commands without invoking the chain', () => {
    75	    const { chain, log } = makeMockChain();
    76	    const result = runDefaultCommand('toggleUnderline', chain);
    77	    expect(result).toBe(false);
    78	    expect(log.calls).toEqual(['focus']);
    79	
    80	    const r2 = makeMockChain();
    81	    expect(runDefaultCommand('setLink', r2.chain)).toBe(false);
    82	    expect(runDefaultCommand('nonsense', r2.chain)).toBe(false);
    83	  });
    84	
    85	  it('every defaultToolbarConfig button id is dispatch-routable', () => {
    86	    for (const button of defaultToolbarConfig.buttons) {
    87	      const { chain, log } = makeMockChain();
    88	      const result = runDefaultCommand(button.command, chain);
    89	      expect(result, `button ${button.id} (command ${button.command}) must dispatch`).toBe(true);
    90	      expect(log.calls.length, `button ${button.id} must invoke chain`).toBeGreaterThan(1);
    91	    }
    92	  });
    93	});
    94	
    95	describe('isDefaultCommandActive', () => {
    96	  it('queries editor.isActive with the matching node/mark name + heading level', () => {
    97	    const isActive = vi.fn().mockReturnValue(true);
    98	    const editor = { isActive } as unknown as Editor;
    99	
   100	    expect(isDefaultCommandActive(editor, 'toggleBold')).toBe(true);
   101	    expect(isActive).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith('bold');
   102	
   103	    isDefaultCommandActive(editor, 'toggleHeading2');
   104	    expect(isActive).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith('heading', { level: 2 });
   105	
   106	    isDefaultCommandActive(editor, 'toggleBulletList');
   107	    expect(isActive).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith('bulletList');
   108	  });
   109	
   110	  it('returns false for unknown commands without querying editor', () => {
   111	    const isActive = vi.fn().mockReturnValue(true);
   112	    const editor = { isActive } as unknown as Editor;
   113	
   114	    expect(isDefaultCommandActive(editor, 'toggleUnderline')).toBe(false);
   115	    expect(isDefaultCommandActive(editor, 'setLink')).toBe(false);
   116	    expect(isActive).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
   117	  });
   118	});

    59	      /at least one of props or content/,
    60	    );

/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba packages/editor-toolbar/CONTRACT.md | sed -n '1,260p'" in /home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# @skb/editor-toolbar Contract
     2	
     3	第一个 editor-* sub-module（Wave 2 Track G1 template）。
     4	后续 `editor-slash-menu` / `editor-drag-handle` 按本包结构 clone（codex-block-generator）。
     5	
     6	## Public surface
     7	
     8	三个 entry：
     9	
    10	- `.` (root barrel) — re-export `./core`
    11	- `./core` — headless 层（无 React / Tiptap import）
    12	  - `toolbarConfigSchema` / `toolbarButtonSchema` — Zod `.strict()`
    13	  - `defaultToolbarConfig` — **9 default buttons**, grouped by Tiptap surface category:
    14	    - **Marks via StarterKit (3)**: `bold` / `italic` / `strike`
    15	    - **Headings via StarterKit (3)**: `h1` / `h2` / `h3` (all dispatch `toggleHeading({level: N})`)
    16	    - **Lists via StarterKit (2)**: `bulletList` / `orderedList`
    17	    - **Inline code via StarterKit (1)**: `code`
    18	  - `findButton(config, id)` / `listCommands(config)` — config 查询工具
    19	  - `buildEditBlockInput(pageSlug, blockId, draft)` — 调 `editBlockInputSchema.parse`，
    20	    把 toolbar 触发的 selection 改动收敛为 Wave 1 命令模式入参（spec §2.6）
    21	  - `defaultCommandBindings` — `Record<buttonId, tiptapChainCommand>` frozen
    22	  - 类型：`ToolbarButton` / `ToolbarConfig`
    23	- `./ui-default` — React + Tiptap 消费者
    24	  - `EditorToolbar` — `BubbleMenu` 浮动工具条；接 `editor: Editor | null` + 可选 `config`
    25	  - `TOOLBAR_ICONS` — SVG 图标 frozen Record（9 项，与 `defaultToolbarConfig` 严格对齐）；按 `button.icon` key 查表
    26	  - `runDefaultCommand(name, chain)` / `isDefaultCommandActive(editor, command)` — 显式
    27	    `switch` 派发器，把命令名映射到真实的 Tiptap chain API 形状
    28	    （e.g. `'toggleHeading1'` → `chain.focus().toggleHeading({level:1}).run()`）；未知命令返回 `false`，不抛错
    29	
    30	## Invariants
    31	
    32	- **Headless 自给**: `core/` 不 import React / Tiptap；仅依赖 `@skb/editor-commands` + `zod`
    33	  （ADR-0003 D1）
    34	- **Schema strictness**: `toolbarConfigSchema` / `toolbarButtonSchema` 一律 `.strict()`，
    35	  unknown key 直接 reject（ADR-0006 item #3）
    36	- **Cross-package consumer = structural identity**: `buildEditBlockInput` 复用
    37	  `@skb/editor-commands` 的 `editBlockInputSchema`，不 inline 重复 refine 逻辑
    38	  （ADR-0006 item #4 + memory `cross_package_consumer_pattern`）
    39	- **Tiptap 命令派发显式枚举**: `runDefaultCommand` / `isDefaultCommandActive`
    40	  用 `switch` 把 `ToolbarButton.command` 字面值映射到 Tiptap chain API。命令字符串 ≠
    41	  Tiptap 方法名（heading 是 `toggleHeading({level:N})` 而非 `toggleHeading1`），动态
    42	  `chain[name]()` 派发会 silent no-op；显式 switch 是该类 silent-bug 的唯一防线。
    43	  **新增按钮三处必须同步**：(1) `defaultToolbarConfig.buttons` 增项，(2)
    44	  `runDefaultCommand` 加 case，(3) `isDefaultCommandActive` 加 case + 同 PR 加 dispatch
    45	  test。`__tests__/toolbar-dispatch.test.ts#every defaultToolbarConfig button id is dispatch-routable`
    46	  自动卡口任何漏 case（ADR-0006 item #1：算法 + 比较器配套）
    47	
    48	- **Prose-extension 对齐**: 默认按钮集合**严格子集**于 `@skb/block-foundation/prose.ts`
    49	  `proseExtensions`（StarterKit + TaskList + Typography + tiptap-markdown）暴露的命令。
    50	  `underline` / `link` 暂不在 prose 扩展中 → **不**进入 `defaultToolbarConfig`，避免
    51	  silent no-op。Wave 3 若 block-foundation 加入 `@tiptap/extension-underline` /
    52	  `@tiptap/extension-link`，本包同 PR 在 `defaultToolbarConfig` 加按钮 + `runDefaultCommand`
    53	  加 case + 加 dispatch test（ADR-0006 item #1 + sister-package alignment）
    54	- **A11y**: 工具条 `role="toolbar"` + `aria-label`；按钮 `aria-pressed` + `aria-label`
    55	  含可选 `shortcut`；图标 `aria-hidden`
    56	- **CSS 变量绑定 design-tokens**: `toolbar.css` 用 `var(--skb-color-*)`；
    57	  consumer 应用 `@skb/design-tokens`（Wave 2 后续 site 集成时引入；本包 CSS 写
    58	  fallback hex 让独立测试可用）
    59	
    60	## Wave 3 future work
    61	
    62	**Underline + link buttons deferred.** `defaultToolbarConfig` ships with 9 buttons (above)
    63	because that's the strict subset of commands `@skb/block-foundation/src/prose.ts` `proseExtensions`
    64	(StarterKit + TaskList + Typography + tiptap-markdown) actually exposes. To enable underline
    65	and link, Wave 3 must do — in this order, in one cross-package PR:
    66	
    67	1. `@skb/block-foundation`: add `@tiptap/extension-underline` (and a link-flow primitive — link
    68	   needs an href-input UX, not a single chain command) to `proseExtensions`; bump deps in
    69	   `package.json`; regenerate `pnpm-lock.yaml`.
    70	2. `@skb/editor-toolbar` (this package, same PR): add the two buttons to `defaultToolbarConfig`,
    71	   add their cases to `runDefaultCommand` + `isDefaultCommandActive`, restore the Underline +
    72	   LinkIcon SVG entries to `TOOLBAR_ICONS`, and update the dispatch test "every default
    73	   button id is dispatch-routable" to cover them.
    74	
    75	Until that PR lands, the dispatch layer fails-safe: `runDefaultCommand('toggleUnderline', …)`
    76	and `runDefaultCommand('setLink', …)` both return `false`. The G1 R2 dispatch tests assert
    77	this fail-safe so accidental re-introduction of those button IDs without the prerequisite
    78	prose-extension work is caught immediately (ADR-0006 item #1).
    79	
    80	## G2 / G3 codex-clone 指引
    81	
    82	`codex-block-generator`（[runbook](../../docs/runbooks/codex-tool-invocations.md#codex-block-generator)）
    83	以本包为 source 模板生成姐妹 sub-module：
    84	
    85	1. **G2 `editor-slash-menu`**: 改 `toolbarConfig` → `slashMenuConfig`（`items: SlashMenuItem[]`，
    86	   每项 `command` 改成 `insert_block` 的 `blockType` + `props`）；改 `buildEditBlockInput`
    87	   → `buildInsertBlockInput`，复用 `commandSchemas.insert_block`；UI 改成下拉列表（可
    88	   保留 BubbleMenu 浮动定位）
    89	2. **G3 `editor-drag-handle`**: 改 `toolbarConfig` → `dragHandleConfig`（仅 1-2 个动作：
    90	   move-up / move-down / drag-start）；UI 改成 block 左侧拖拽 handle；`buildMoveBlockInput`
    91	   复用 `commandSchemas.move_block`
    92	3. **保持目录结构同形**: `core/{*-config.ts, command-bindings.ts, index.ts}` +
    93	   `ui-default/{*.tsx, *.css, icons/, dispatch.ts}` + `__tests__/{*-config.test.ts, *-dispatch.test.ts}`
    94	   （schema-validation 套件 ≥4 用例 + dispatch 套件覆盖每个 default 按钮 → Tiptap 真实 chain 形状）
    95	4. **Dispatch 测试模板**: 每个 sub-module 必须配 `*-dispatch.test.ts`，用 `Proxy`-mock 的
    96	   `ChainedCommands` 校验：(a) 每个 `defaultConfig` 按钮都能 dispatch 成功；(b) 命令名
    97	   到真实 chain 方法 + 参数（如 `{level: N}`）的映射；(c) 未知命令早返回。
    98	   `runDefaultCommand` switch 与 `defaultConfig` 之间的 drift 由 "every defaultConfig button

     1	# @skb/editor-commands Contract
     2	
     3	## Public surface
     4	
     5	- `commandSchemas` — 7 个 Zod 命令 schema（discriminated by `type` literal）：
     6	  - `create_page` / `delete_page`
     7	  - `insert_block` / `edit_block` / `delete_block` / `move_block`
     8	  - `update_frontmatter`
     9	- `parseCommand(input)` — 校验任意 unknown 输入并返回 discriminated `Command`
    10	- `slugSchema` — `[a-z0-9-]+` 正则；用于全部 `pageSlug` 字段，防止 path traversal / 大小写混乱（apps/api/CONVENTIONS.md §1）
    11	- `editBlockInputSchema` — Zod schema for `edit_block` agent tool input. Composes `editBlockBase.omit({ type: true }).refine(at-least-one-of-props-or-content)`. Exported for cross-package consumption by `@skb/agent-tools` (single source of truth for the refine logic; both `parseCommand` 路径与 `toolSchemas.edit_block.input` 共用此 schema 与 predicate)。重命名 / 删除需 ADR。
    12	- `editBlockHasChange(v)` — predicate paired with `editBlockInputSchema`；同时被 `commandSchemas.edit_block` 与 `editBlockInputSchema` 的 `.refine()` 复用，保证二者无 drift。
    13	- `Command` / `CommandType` / `ParseCommandResult` 类型工具
    14	
    15	每个 schema 是 Zod，可同时验证 UI 操作产生的命令与 agent 工具调用产生的命令。
    16	
    17	## Invariants（spec §2.6 关键不变量）
    18	
    19	- **所有编辑器变更必须经此层** —— UI 操作 / agent 工具 / 脚本批改皆同
    20	- 命令是不可变数据；执行器（Wave 3 editor-shell + apps/api）才有副作用
    21	- 命令字段一律 camelCase（含 `pageSlug`，全部 7 命令统一）；type 字段值一律 snake_case
    22	- **FROZEN：所有 object schema 用 `.strict()`，未知字段一律 reject**（防止 agent 误传、防止字段名漂移）
    23	- **Frontmatter schema authority**：从 `@skb/content-types` 导入 `frontmatterSchema`，但本包消费时一律加 `.strict()` 防止 agent typo 被静默 strip（R3 erratum）。`create_page.frontmatter` 用 `frontmatterSchema.strict()`；`update_frontmatter.patch` 用 `frontmatterSchema.strict().partial()`。不重新定义结构。
    24	- **Slug schema authority**：`slugSchema` 在本包定义并导出（regex `/^[a-z0-9-]+$/`），`@skb/agent-tools` 复用同一 schema 供 `read_page` 等 tool 输入校验，与 apps/api/CONVENTIONS.md §1 slug 约束对齐。
    25	
    26	## Modifying this file
    27	
    28	`commandSchemas` / `parseCommand` / `slugSchema` / `editBlockInputSchema` / `editBlockHasChange` 的任何字段或方法增删改一律需 ADR — **包括添加新命令、添加 optional 字段、放宽 `.strict()`**。spec §2.3 把接口包归为"改动需 ADR；全员同步"；spec §2.6 把 editor-commands 列为 Phase 1 必建的命令模式 surface（agent integration 基石）。
    29	
    30	原因：editor-commands 是 agent-tools 的源头（mutating tools 1:1 wrap commandSchemas via `.omit({type:true})`；edit_block 路径走 `editBlockInputSchema` 共享 predicate）；通过 agent-tools 接到 Phase 2b apps/api dispatch；任何 schema 变化都要协调 4+ 个层（editor-shell / agent-tools / apps/api / agent prompt 模板）。

     1	# @skb/block-foundation Contract
     2	
     3	## Public surface
     4	
     5	- `BlockRegistry` class — 双层注册表（ADR-0003）
     6	  - `registerCore(core)` / `registerUI(ui)`
     7	  - `getCore(name)` / `listCores()`
     8	  - `getUI(coreName, uiId?)` / `listUIs(coreName)`
     9	- `defineCore(def)` / `defineUI(def)` factory helpers
    10	- `BlockCoreDefinition<T>` / `BlockUIDefinition<T>` / `BlockViewProps<T>` interfaces
    11	- `BlockKind = 'prose' | 'component' | 'render' | 'viz'` （四分类，Wave 2 由 2→4 additive 扩展）
    12	  - `'prose'` — Wave 1 default for headless prose blocks (无 MDX 组件，由 `proseExtensions` 提供 markdown 行为)
    13	  - `'component'` — Wave 1 for MDX-component blocks（block-callout / block-code / block-image — Track C simple-block-eng）
    14	  - `'render'` — **Wave 2 NEW** for hand-crafted render blocks consuming external runtime authority（block-math KaTeX / block-pdf react-pdf — Track D render-block-eng）
    15	  - `'viz'` — **Wave 2 NEW** for visualization blocks consuming heavy runtime libs（block-jupyter Pyodide / block-nn-viz TensorFlow.js / block-agent-flow React Flow — Track E viz-block-eng / kernel consumer）
    16	- `proseExtensions` — Tiptap 扩展数组，提供全部 markdown 行为
    17	
    18	## Invariants
    19	
    20	- `BlockKind` 四分不可破坏：新 block 必须明确归属 `prose` / `component` / `render` / `viz` 之一。Wave 1 仅 `prose` + `component` 二分；Wave 2 additive 扩展加 `render` + `viz`（由 `render-block-eng` 与 `viz-block-eng` 工种 own，与 agent-contract.md Tier 1 worker 分工一致）。kind 定义不可被进一步窄化（不允许把 `render` 重新归并入 `component`），扩展再加 kind 需 ADR
    21	- Prose blocks 零自写代码——任何看似需要新 prose block 的场景应通过组合 `proseExtensions` 内现有扩展或追加单条 Tiptap 扩展实现，不再加 prose-kind block
    22	- `BlockCoreDefinition.mdxComponent` 必须 PascalCase，且与 MDX 文件 import 中使用的名字一致
    23	- Core 与 UI 物理分离（ADR-0003）：core 不允许 import 任何 React/Tiptap 视觉 API；UI 必须 import core（不允许 inline 重复 schema）
    24	- 同 core 多 UI 时 `getUI(name)` 取首个注册：约定首个 uiId 为 `'default'`；adopter 在 register 顺序上需谨慎
    25	- **Defensive copy**：`listCores()` 与 `listUIs(coreName)` 每次返回 fresh array。Caller 修改返回值不影响 registry 内部 state。Implementation: `src/registry.ts:84` (`listCores`) + `src/registry.ts:96` (`listUIs`)；regression: `src/__tests__/registry.test.ts` "listCores returns a defensive copy" + "listUIs returns a defensive copy"
    26	- **Idempotent register**：`registerCore` 同 `name` 第二次调用 throws `Duplicate core name: <name>`；`registerUI` 同 `(coreName, uiId)` 第二次 throws `Duplicate UI registration: core=<coreName> uiId=<uiId>`。避免 silent override；实现见 `src/registry.ts:60-63` + `src/registry.ts:71-75`
    27	- **Schema strictness**：每个 `BlockCoreDefinition.propsSchema` 必须是 `z.object(...).strict()`；嵌套 `ZodObject` 同样必须 `.strict()`（[ADR-0006](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0006-asymmetry-audit-checklist.md) item #3 trip-hazard：`.strict()` 不传播）。当前类型仅约束到 `ZodTypeAny`（type-narrow 到 `ZodObject` 待 Wave 3）；Wave 2 行为约定 + reviewer + RFC.md §1 显式提醒。未 `.strict()` 不会 throw，但 `propsSchema.parse(input)` 会 silently strip unknown keys，引发 contract drift
    28	- **Serialize / parse hook ownership**：MDX serialize/parse 由各 `block-*` 包的 `core/` own（ADR-0003 D1+D2），不在 `block-foundation` 注册。`block-foundation` 仅 own `BlockCoreDefinition` 形状（含 `mdxComponent` 字符串）；`mdx-bridge` 通过 `mdxJsxFlowElement.name` 字符串路由到对应 block 的 export（命名约定 verb-as-prefix：`serialize<BlockName>` / `parse<BlockName>`，与 mdx-bridge 现有 `mdxToTiptap` / `tiptapToMdx` 同 style）。详见 RFC.md §"5. Serialize / parse hook ownership"
    29	
    30	## Forward-compat consumers (Wave 2+)
    31	
    32	Wave 2 中段当 `BlockRegistry` 开始用 `@skb/content-types` 的 schema 验证
    33	注册的 block props（如跨 block 共享的 frontmatter / metadata 形状），届时
    34	`@skb/content-types` 将被同 PR 加入 `package.json#dependencies` +
    35	`tsconfig.json#references`，并出现源码 `import { ... } from '@skb/content-types'`。
    36	
    37	当前 Wave 1 close 状态：源码无 content-types import，按

    39	（dead-dep policy = tighten），dep 与 ref 均未声明；forward-compat 意图
    40	仅在本 prose 段表达，不在 package.json / tsconfig 占位。

    45	for the first real consumer 在 [RFC.md](./RFC.md) — 第一个 `block-*` PR
    46	(Task C1 / C2) 必须 reference 此 RFC；后续 `block-*` PR 沿用相同模式。
    47	RFC 是 consumer-facing 教程，CONTRACT.md 是 public API 形状 + 关键不变量
    48	的权威；二者补充而非重叠（[ADR-0008 D2](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0008-wave-2-entry-policies.md)）。

docs/decisions/ADR-0006-asymmetry-audit-checklist.md:60:6. **Sister-file documentation audit**: After updating one CONTRACT.md (or any document that describes a code surface), audit ALL related CONTRACT.md files in the package family for sister-file drift. Two CONTRACT.md files that describe two halves of the same contract must use **identical terminology** for the shared identifiers (function names, schema names, refine predicate names). (Wave 1 evidence: instance #5 — `editor-commands/CONTRACT.md` updated to cite `editBlockInputSchema` while `agent-tools/CONTRACT.md` still referred to the old shape.)
docs/decisions/ADR-0006-asymmetry-audit-checklist.md:84:   **Pitfall extension — `pnpm-lock.yaml` is a generated artifact too (sub-form C below)**: when the authority is `package.json` (any dependency add / remove / version bump / `@types/*` shift), `pnpm install` regenerates `pnpm-lock.yaml` as a deterministic derived artifact. Skipping `git add pnpm-lock.yaml` produces the same class of failure as skipping `git add .claude/agents/*.md`: working tree has the synced lockfile but the **index** still holds HEAD's stale lockfile, so the bundle ships `package.json` ahead of its lockfile. CI runs `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` from the bundled state, sees the stale lockfile disagreeing with the new `package.json`, and fails install. The same `git diff --cached --stat -- pnpm-lock.yaml` canonical verification applies. (Wave 1 erratum commit `e15ec36`; codified as sub-form C below.)
docs/decisions/ADR-0006-asymmetry-audit-checklist.md:88:   **Wave 1 evidence**: **instance #8** — ADR-0006 v1 commit codified reviewer-prompt mandates but did not update `agent-contract.md` / regenerate `.claude/agents/code-reviewer.md` / regenerate `.claude/agents/pr-gate.md` / cross-link `team-operations.md` / regenerate `docs/review-checklist.md`; pr-gate caught the dogfood failure during R1 review of the close-ceremony bundle, forcing a v2 fix. **instance #12 (sub-form A — pre-regeneration omission)** — v5 bundle updated authority files (`agent-contract.md`, generator code, `team-operations.md` cross-link) but had not run the generator at all post-edit; the 3 regenerated outputs were stale at HEAD content; pr-gate caught this during R2 review, forcing a v6 fix. **instance #12 (sub-form B — staging-vs-working-tree confusion)** — v6 bundle ran the generator (so working tree had new content) but did NOT `git add` the regenerated outputs; the git **index** still held HEAD blob hashes for the 3 generated files; orchestrator confused `git status --short` "M" prefix (working-tree-vs-index) with index-vs-HEAD; pr-gate caught this via `git ls-files --stage` blob-hash comparison during R3 review, forcing the v7 fix that explicitly stages with `git add` + verifies via `git diff --cached`. **instance #12 (sub-form C — lockfile is a generated-from-authority artifact)** — Wave 1 close ceremony commit `e15ec36` ("fix(deps): commit pnpm-lock.yaml for @tailwindcss/typography (lockfile drift)"): the design-tokens erratum (commit `fdc86a8`) added the `@tailwindcss/typography` dependency to `package.json` but did not stage the regenerated `pnpm-lock.yaml`; CI install used the pre-update lockfile, drifted against the new `package.json`, and failed `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile`. Resolution: commit `e15ec36` staged the lockfile separately. Lesson codified: when the authority is `package.json` (any dependency add / remove / version bump), `pnpm-lock.yaml` is the consumer-side generated artifact and must ride the same bundle — the class equivalence to `.claude/agents/*.md` regeneration is exact (deterministic derive + git-index staging required). The four sub-forms of the same item #8 class — pre-regeneration omission, post-regeneration commit-staging confusion, conceptually-correct-but-operationally-incomplete fix, AND lockfile-as-generated-artifact — together motivate the explicit operational sequence and `git diff --cached` canonical verification command codified above.

Wave 2 close ceremony reviews instances and adds items as needed.

/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba docs/decisions/ADR-0006-asymmetry-audit-checklist.md | sed -n '1,130p'" in /home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# ADR-0006: Cross-location asymmetry-audit checklist (process rule)
     2	
     3	| 字段 | 值                                    |
     4	| ---- | ------------------------------------- |
     5	| 状态 | accepted                              |
     6	| 日期 | 2026-04-30                            |
     7	| 作者 | orchestrator (Claude Opus 4.7 1M ctx) |
     8	| 触发 | Wave 1 codex 5.5 reviews caught 12 instances of the same meta-class across 6 tracks + 5 close-ceremony fix iterations (8 distinct items + 4 close-ceremony reinforcements of items #6 and #8) |
     9	| 替代 | 不替代任何 ADR；扩展 [ADR-0001](ADR-0001-stack-selection.md) §3.2 review chain |
    10	
    11	## Context
    12	
    13	Wave 1 implementation produced strong empirical evidence that **a single bug class** dominates the failure mode at line-level review:
    14	
    15	> **Cross-location asymmetry**: a value, schema, algorithm, or invariant exists in two or more places, and an edit to one location is not symmetrically applied to all peers, so behavior diverges silently.
    16	
    17	Across 6 tracks + 5 close-ceremony fix iterations, codex 5.5 (`pr-gate`) caught **12 instances** of this meta-class (8 distinct failure-mode items + 4 close-ceremony reinforcements of items #6 and #8). Every PR that lasted ≥ 2 review rounds hit at least one instance. The pattern is so consistent that linear "fix the cited blocker, ship v_n+1" iteration would not have caught the second-order asymmetry; codex specifically catches the symmetric counterpart that the worker missed.
    18	
    19	### Wave 1 empirical record (12 instances)
    20	
    21	| # | Track | Round | Asymmetry pair | Bug behavior |
    22	|---|---|---|---|---|
    23	| 1 | Track B (block-foundation + content-types) | R1 → fixed v3 | `listUIs` mutability vs `listCores` defensive copy | Caller mutation of return value polluted registry singleton |
    24	| 2 | Track D (apps/api) | R2 → fixed v4 | RFC 7235 `WWW-Authenticate` on framework-raised 401 vs custom `SkbError` 401 | 4 of 5 401 paths emitted spec-mandated header; the custom-error path silently dropped it |
    25	| 3 | Track F (editor-commands) | R3 → fixed v4 | `.strict()` on top-level Zod object vs nested `frontmatterSchema` | Top-level rejected unknown keys; nested still silently stripped them |
    26	| 4 | Track C (mdx-bridge) | R2 → fixed v4 | `marksEqual` link comparator: `href` only vs `href + title` | Same href, different titles silently merged into one link with the first title preserved |
    27	| 5 | Track F v5 | R3 → fixed v6 | One CONTRACT.md updated to reflect code; sister CONTRACT.md (`agent-tools`) had stale wording | Documentation-vs-code drift in the file the worker did not edit |
    28	| 6 | Track A | R1 → fixed v2 | FOUC inline IIFE saved-value algorithm + content schema | Truthy-coerce diverges from strict-whitelist for invalid / case-mismatched values; inline `z.object` redefinition diverges from `frontmatterSchema` authority |
    29	| 7 | Track A v2 | R2 → fixed v3 | FOUC inline IIFE try/catch scope | Wide try/catch in IIFE swallowed `matchMedia` exceptions while authority's narrow try/catch propagated; storage-throws scenarios produced light→dark hydration flash |
    30	| 8 | Wave 1 close bundle v1 | R1 → fixed v2 | ADR-0006 mandated reviewer-prompt updates vs actual `agent-contract.md` / generated `.claude/agents/*.md` / `docs/review-checklist.md` / `team-operations.md` content | The close-ceremony commit codified a process rule that mandates downstream behavior; consumer-side artifacts (reviewer prompts + runbook) had not been updated to embed the rule, so Wave 2 reviewers would not have seen it |
    31	| 9 | Wave 1 close bundle v2 | R2 → fixed v3 | ADR-0006:81 within-document drift "7 distinct instances" | Single bullet missed during the v1→v2 count sweep — sister-bullet variant of item #6 within a single document |
    32	| 10 | Wave 1 close bundle v3 | R3 → fixed v4 | ADR-0006:117 within-document drift "7 empirical instances + 18+ erratum candidates" (both numbers stale) | Second sister-bullet drift in the same file, a different bullet — proved per-citation fixes are themselves item #6 traps; comprehensive sweep at fix time is the robust pattern |
    33	| 11 | Wave 1 close bundle v4 (informational) | R4 (informational) → fixed v5 | structure baseline:66 "× 7 endpoints" while apps/api has 4 routes / 11 tests | Pre-existing content-accuracy drift in the structure baseline document (authored at v1) — content number mismatched real code; surfaced during the broader 11th-class hunt |
    34	| 12 | Wave 1 close bundle v5 | R2 (pr-gate) → fixed v6 | Regenerated `.claude/agents/code-reviewer.md` + `.claude/agents/pr-gate.md` + `docs/review-checklist.md` were not staged in the bundle commit — authority + generator code updated, but generator outputs remained at pre-update HEAD content | Sub-form of item #8: regenerating an artifact and `git add`'ing it are separate steps; a bundle that updates authority + generator without staging the regenerated outputs is silently stale. Motivated the "verify by clean second-run" step now part of item #8. |
    35	
    36	Codex's structured review consistently caught these with three patterns:
    37	
    38	1. After fixing one location, **codex audits all peer locations** (not just the cited site)
    39	2. After a value/schema acquires an attribute, **codex audits every comparator/serialization function** for that attribute
    40	3. After a "happy path" fix, **codex audits the exception path** for the same equivalence
    41	
    42	These three habits collectively define the process rule below.
    43	
    44	## Decision
    45	
    46	Adopt an **8-point asymmetry-audit checklist** as a mandatory part of `code-reviewer` (Codex 5.3-spark, default cheap) and `pr-gate` (Codex 5.5, deep) review prompts. Reviewers MUST work through every applicable item before declaring PASS.
    47	
    48	### The 8 items
    49	
    50	1. **Field/attribute audit**: After adding a field to a data shape (Zod schema, Pydantic model, TS interface, mdast attrs, etc.), audit every comparator / equality / serialization / canonicalization function that handles this shape. (Wave 1 evidence: instance #4 — `marksEqual` link branch missed `title` when serialize emitted it.)
    51	
    52	2. **Status code audit**: After adding a status code to one HTTP handler (or, generally, an exit/error code to one branch), audit every other handler emitting the same code. RFC-mandated headers (RFC 7235 `WWW-Authenticate` on 401, RFC 7231 `Allow` on 405) attach to **the status code itself**, not the framework code path that emitted it. (Wave 1 evidence: instance #2 — custom `SkbError` 401 path missed `WWW-Authenticate`.)
    53	
    54	3. **Strictness audit**: After adding `.strict()` (Zod) / `extra='forbid'` (Pydantic) at one level of an object schema, audit every nested object/sub-schema. The strictness setting **does not propagate**; nested objects must opt in independently. (Wave 1 evidence: instance #3 — top-level `.strict()` shipped while nested `frontmatterSchema` still silently stripped unknown keys.)
    55	
    56	4. **Consumer schema audit**: After defining a schema in a single-authority package (e.g. `@skb/content-types/frontmatterSchema`), audit every consumer for inline duplicates or inline redefinitions that should `import` instead. The "deferred swap" pattern — "I'll inline now and import later" — is a contract break that compounds over time. (Wave 1 evidence: instance #6 part 2 — apps/site/content.config.ts inlined `z.object(...)` rather than importing `frontmatterSchema`.)
    57	
    58	5. **Algorithm + runtime constant replication audit**: When an algorithm or runtime constant is replicated outside its authority (e.g. inline FOUC scripts, login challenge derivation, pre-hydration boot logic that cannot import from packages), audit every consumer-side replica for byte-equivalent reproduction. The replica must register a **regression test** that asserts equivalence against the authority across a representative input corpus. (Wave 1 evidence: instance #6 part 1 — FOUC IIFE truthy-coerce vs `getInitialTheme()` strict-whitelist; remediated with `apps/site/src/__tests__/fouc-script.test.ts` 16-row corpus.)
    59	
    60	6. **Sister-file documentation audit**: After updating one CONTRACT.md (or any document that describes a code surface), audit ALL related CONTRACT.md files in the package family for sister-file drift. Two CONTRACT.md files that describe two halves of the same contract must use **identical terminology** for the shared identifiers (function names, schema names, refine predicate names). (Wave 1 evidence: instance #5 — `editor-commands/CONTRACT.md` updated to cite `editBlockInputSchema` while `agent-tools/CONTRACT.md` still referred to the old shape.)
    61	
    62	7. **Exception-scope equivalence audit**: When replicating an algorithm + its exception handling across files, audit BOTH the happy-path semantics AND the `try/catch` scope between authority and replica. Narrow vs wide `try/catch` can produce divergent fall-through behavior even with identical happy-path logic. Specifically: when `localStorage` / `sessionStorage` / `IndexedDB` / `matchMedia` / any browser API in the algorithm path can throw, the `try/catch` MUST wrap ONLY the throwing call, with the fall-through branch (matchMedia / system default / DOM apply) running AFTER the catch handler — not inside it. (Wave 1 evidence: instance #7 — FOUC IIFE wide-scope try/catch swallowed matchMedia exceptions; remediated with narrow try/catch + 2 storage-throws regression tests.)
    63	
    64	8. **Authority-document → generated/consumed surface audit**: When updating an authority document (ADR, spec, `agent-contract.md`, runbook, CONVENTIONS) **OR a dependency authority (`package.json`)** that mandates behavior in downstream consumers, audit every consumer-side replica/generated artifact for synchronized update **AND staged-in-the-git-index in the same bundle**. Specifically: an ADR section that says "X file MUST be updated to do Y" requires the same commit to update X and provide a way to verify the synchronization. Generated artifacts (e.g. `docs/review-checklist.md` and `.claude/agents/*.md` derived from `agent-contract.md`; **`pnpm-lock.yaml` derived from `package.json`** — see sub-form C below) MUST be regenerated via the documented codegen step (`pnpm generate:configs` / `pnpm install`) AND **explicitly `git add`'d** so the git **index** (not just the working tree) contains the new content; runbooks (`docs/runbooks/team-operations.md`) MUST be cross-linked; Codex/Claude subagent prompt files MUST embed or reference the new rule.
    65	
    66	   **Operational sequence** (do not skip steps):
    67	   ```bash
    68	   # 1. Edit authority + generator code
    69	   # 2. Run codegen
    70	   pnpm generate:configs
    71	   # 3. Stage regenerated outputs (CRITICAL — see Pitfall below)
    72	   git add <regenerated/path/foo.md> <regenerated/path/bar.md>
    73	   # 4. Verify index reflects content (NOT just working tree):
    74	   git diff --cached --stat -- <regenerated paths>
    75	   #    → expects non-empty diff with the expected line counts
    76	   # 5. Verify idempotence: re-run codegen + confirm working tree matches index:
    77	   pnpm generate:configs
    78	   git status --short -- <regenerated paths>
    79	   #    → expects column-1 'M' (staged) + column-2 blank (working tree clean)
    80	   ```
    81	
    82	   **Pitfall (this is the load-bearing step)**: `git status --short` shows two columns: column 1 is index-vs-HEAD; column 2 is working-tree-vs-index. After running the generator without `git add`, you see `' M'` (space + M) — **working tree differs from index, but index still matches HEAD**. `git commit` only commits the index. Skipping `git add` means the regenerated content is in your working tree but NOT in the next commit. The bundle would land authority + generator-code changes WITHOUT the regenerated outputs, leaving consumers (Wave 2 reviewers reading `.claude/agents/*.md`) at pre-update content. Use `git diff --cached` (not `git status`) as the canonical staging-verification command — `--cached` shows index-vs-HEAD only, which is what `git commit` will produce.
    83	
    84	   **Pitfall extension — `pnpm-lock.yaml` is a generated artifact too (sub-form C below)**: when the authority is `package.json` (any dependency add / remove / version bump / `@types/*` shift), `pnpm install` regenerates `pnpm-lock.yaml` as a deterministic derived artifact. Skipping `git add pnpm-lock.yaml` produces the same class of failure as skipping `git add .claude/agents/*.md`: working tree has the synced lockfile but the **index** still holds HEAD's stale lockfile, so the bundle ships `package.json` ahead of its lockfile. CI runs `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` from the bundled state, sees the stale lockfile disagreeing with the new `package.json`, and fails install. The same `git diff --cached --stat -- pnpm-lock.yaml` canonical verification applies. (Wave 1 erratum commit `e15ec36`; codified as sub-form C below.)
    85	
    86	   Generated-from-authority files carry their own header comment ("GENERATED FROM agent-contract.md — DO NOT EDIT BY HAND") OR YAML frontmatter declaring authority source — both forms are acceptable. `pnpm-lock.yaml` lacks an explicit header (the format is fixed by pnpm) but is implicitly declared by `pnpm install`'s contract — its authority is `package.json` + the workspace `pnpm-workspace.yaml`.
    87	
    88	   **Wave 1 evidence**: **instance #8** — ADR-0006 v1 commit codified reviewer-prompt mandates but did not update `agent-contract.md` / regenerate `.claude/agents/code-reviewer.md` / regenerate `.claude/agents/pr-gate.md` / cross-link `team-operations.md` / regenerate `docs/review-checklist.md`; pr-gate caught the dogfood failure during R1 review of the close-ceremony bundle, forcing a v2 fix. **instance #12 (sub-form A — pre-regeneration omission)** — v5 bundle updated authority files (`agent-contract.md`, generator code, `team-operations.md` cross-link) but had not run the generator at all post-edit; the 3 regenerated outputs were stale at HEAD content; pr-gate caught this during R2 review, forcing a v6 fix. **instance #12 (sub-form B — staging-vs-working-tree confusion)** — v6 bundle ran the generator (so working tree had new content) but did NOT `git add` the regenerated outputs; the git **index** still held HEAD blob hashes for the 3 generated files; orchestrator confused `git status --short` "M" prefix (working-tree-vs-index) with index-vs-HEAD; pr-gate caught this via `git ls-files --stage` blob-hash comparison during R3 review, forcing the v7 fix that explicitly stages with `git add` + verifies via `git diff --cached`. **instance #12 (sub-form C — lockfile is a generated-from-authority artifact)** — Wave 1 close ceremony commit `e15ec36` ("fix(deps): commit pnpm-lock.yaml for @tailwindcss/typography (lockfile drift)"): the design-tokens erratum (commit `fdc86a8`) added the `@tailwindcss/typography` dependency to `package.json` but did not stage the regenerated `pnpm-lock.yaml`; CI install used the pre-update lockfile, drifted against the new `package.json`, and failed `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile`. Resolution: commit `e15ec36` staged the lockfile separately. Lesson codified: when the authority is `package.json` (any dependency add / remove / version bump), `pnpm-lock.yaml` is the consumer-side generated artifact and must ride the same bundle — the class equivalence to `.claude/agents/*.md` regeneration is exact (deterministic derive + git-index staging required). The four sub-forms of the same item #8 class — pre-regeneration omission, post-regeneration commit-staging confusion, conceptually-correct-but-operationally-incomplete fix, AND lockfile-as-generated-artifact — together motivate the explicit operational sequence and `git diff --cached` canonical verification command codified above.
    89	
    90	### Wave 2 prep notes (forward-compatibility application)
    91	
    92	The 8 items have specific Wave 2 trip-hazards already identified by Wave 1's review record:
    93	
    94	- **Item #1 + Wave 2 attr-bearing marks**: when the first attr-bearing mark beyond `link` is added to mdx-bridge (e.g. `highlight: {color}`, `kbd: {style}`), the worker MUST simultaneously update `wrapMark` AND `marksEqual` AND add a fixture exercising attr-equality + attr-distinction. Default `return true` in `marksEqual` is a silent-drop trap for any attr-bearing mark. (See pr-gate Track C v4 R3 caveat.)
    95	
    96	- **Item #2 + Wave 2 new error categories**: when Wave 2+ adds new HTTP error categories (e.g. `auth-rate-limit`, `kernel-timeout`), each new `SkbError` subclass MUST emit the same RFC-mandated headers as framework-raised peers. Add a unit test asserting header presence per error type. (See `apps/api/CONVENTIONS.md` §5 Pitfall + Pitfall continued.)
    97	
    98	- **Item #5 + Wave 2 client-side replications**: when apps/site adds new client-side algorithm replications (theme variants beyond light/dark, locale negotiation pre-hydration, etc.), the worker MUST audit both saved-value semantics AND try/catch scope against authority. (See `packages/design-tokens/CONTRACT.md` Inverse-direction obligation invariant.)
    99	
   100	- **Item #7 + future inline scripts**: ANY future `<script is:inline>` block in the project that needs pre-hydration replication of a package algorithm MUST follow the FOUC template (narrow try/catch + corpus regression test).
   101	
   102	## Consequences
   103	
   104	### Positive
   105	
   106	- **Catch the failure mode proactively**: Wave 1 evidence shows reviewer-applied checklist would have caught all 12 instances on the first review round (including the close-ceremony dogfood instance #8 + the 4 fix-loop reinforcements), eliminating 12 round-trip cycles
   107	- **Reviewer prompt clarity**: code-reviewer + pr-gate codex profile prompts gain a concrete, enumerable checklist rather than the implicit "look for symmetric counterparts" expectation
   108	- **Worker self-audit**: workers gain a pre-PR checklist they can run before reporting `ready-for-review`, catching their own asymmetries (memory: `feedback_verification_fresh_state.md` showed the verification-rigor lesson; this checklist is the analogous rigor at code-design level)
   109	- **Empirical grounding**: 12 distinct instances spread across 6 tracks + 5 close-ceremony fix iterations make this rule data-driven, not a "best practice" speculation; instances #9-12 reinforce items #6 + #8 specifically through close-ceremony fix-loop evidence
   110	
   111	### Costs
   112	
   113	- **Review-round time**: applying 8-point audit at every review adds ~5-10 minutes per round of code-reviewer + pr-gate runtime. Acceptable trade-off given Wave 1 round-counts (Track F: 5 rounds; Track A: 3 rounds; Track C: 4 rounds; Track D: 4 rounds) — front-loading review thoroughness reduces total cycles
   114	- **Worker prompt overhead**: each spawn prompt now needs a reference to this ADR + the 8-point checklist. Marginal cost.
   115	- **False-positive risk**: items that don't apply (e.g. #2 "status code" doesn't apply to TS-only packages) need to be triaged and skipped. Reviewer profile prompts must explicitly say "skip items not applicable to this PR's scope" to avoid bloat.
   116	
   117	### Risks (mitigated)
   118	
   119	- **Risk**: workers might mechanically check items without thinking, producing false confidence. **Mitigation**: each item enumerates concrete failure modes from Wave 1, not abstract heuristics — easier to apply
   120	- **Risk**: this list might grow unbounded as Wave 2+ surfaces new asymmetry classes. **Mitigation**: ADR amendments are cheap; each new instance produces an ADR-0006 erratum capturing item #N+1. Wave 2 close ceremony reviews instances and adds items as needed.
   121	
   122	## Implementation
   123	
   124	### Reviewer prompt updates
   125	
   126	`code-reviewer` and `pr-gate` profile prompts MUST be updated to:
   127	
   128	1. State the 8-point checklist verbatim (or by reference to this ADR)
   129	2. Require the reviewer's verdict structure to include "asymmetry-audit applied: items {1..8} verdicts: ..."
   130	3. Explicitly mark FAIL if any applicable item is left un-audited

     1	# ADR-0008: Wave 2 entry policies — dead-dep + block-foundation interface freeze
     2	
     3	| 字段 | 值                                                                                                                                                                |
     4	| ---- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
     5	| 状态 | accepted                                                                                                                                                          |

     8	| 触发 | structure-auditor Wave 1 close baseline §7 推荐 #1（dead-dep policy）+ #2（block-foundation interface freeze）；ADR-0002 D3 erratum #19（F1）+ #20（F2）+ #21（block-foundation RFC） |
     9	| 替代 | 不替代任何 ADR；扩展 [ADR-0001](ADR-0001-stack-selection.md) §1.2/§1.8 monorepo 规则 + [ADR-0003](ADR-0003-headless-presentational-split.md) D2 双层接口        |

    11	## Context
    12	
    13	`docs/audits/structure-2026-04-29-wave-1.md` §7 提出 3 个结构关注点；其中 #1
    14	（dead-dep policy）+ #2（block-foundation interface freeze）必须在 Wave 2 第一个
    15	`block-*` PR 之前 lock，否则会让 block-callout 入门 PR 同时承担"消费 contract"+
    16	"决议 dep policy" 双重负担，触发 R-round inflation（Wave 1 Track F 5 轮 + Track A
    17	3 轮的同源教训）。
    18	
    19	ADR-0002 D3 已记录两处 forward-compat dead-dep：
    20	
    21	- **F1 — `packages/mdx-bridge`** `package.json#dependencies` 列出
    22	  `@skb/block-foundation` + `@skb/content-types`，源码零 import；
    23	  `tsconfig.json#references` 留空（即 package.json 与 tsconfig 不一致）。
    24	- **F2 — `packages/block-foundation`** `package.json#dependencies` 列出
    25	  `@skb/content-types`，源码零 import；`tsconfig.json#references` 已含
    26	  `{ "path": "../content-types" }`（即 package.json 与 tsconfig 一致但都是 dead）。
    27	
    28	structure-auditor §7 推荐 #1 给出两条互斥路径：
    29	
    30	- **Option A（tighten）**：每个 `package.json#dependencies/peerDependencies` 中的
    31	  `@skb/*` 必须对应至少一个源码 `from '@skb/<pkg>'` import；
    32	  CONTRACT.md 可在 prose 段表达 forward-compat 意图，但 package.json 不占位。
    33	- **Option B（relax）**：package.json 可 forward-declare；
    34	  `tsconfig.json#references` 必须与 `package.json` 一致；CONTRACT.md 必须解释。
    35	
    36	structure-auditor §7 推荐 #2 提出 block-foundation MDX renderer interface freeze：
    37	Wave 2 brings the first `block-callout` package (per agent-contract `simple-block-eng`)
    38	作为 `block-foundation` 双层 API 第一个真实 consumer；任何接口 gap 会在
    39	block-callout review 阶段反复返工。
    40	
    41	## Decision
    42	
    43	### D1：dead-dep policy = Option A（tighten）
    44	
    45	**规则**：每个 `package.json#dependencies` / `peerDependencies` 中的 `@skb/*`
    46	工作区依赖**必须**对应至少一个源码 `from '@skb/<pkg>'` import。
    47	forward-compat 意图（"Wave N+1 will consume X"）可在 CONTRACT.md prose 段表达，
    48	但**不在 package.json 占位**。
    49	
    50	**理由（Option A 优于 Option B）**：
    51	
    52	1. **audit invariant 更简洁**：dead-dep = bug，结构审计不必为
    53	   "forward-compat 是合法 dead-dep" 维护特例；structure-auditor 月度可机械化
    54	   `grep` 检查（"package.json `@skb/*` ↔ source import 一一对应"），
    55	   比 CONTRACT 文本对比可靠。
    56	2. **YAGNI**：mdx-bridge / block-foundation 真用上时再加 dep 是同包改动 + 同 PR
    57	   单点 churn；预先占位换来的"少改一行 package.json"不抵 audit 复杂度。
    58	3. **CONTRACT prose 仍可表达意图**：authoring intent 不丢，只是不再让
    59	   `package.json` 同时承担 "runtime fact" + "future plan" 双重语义。
    60	4. **Phase 3 npm publish 反向证据弱**：当前 phase 全部 `private: true`
    61	   workspace 包，npm publish 顾虑是 Phase 3 议题；届时若需 forward-compat 的
    62	   package.json 形态可在 ADR-N 重新审视；当前不应被 hypothetical future
    63	   requirement 绑架（CLAUDE.md hard rule "Don't design for hypothetical future"）。
    64	
    65	**具体执行（D1 应用范围限于 Wave 1 已识别的两处）**：
    66	
    67	| 包                             | 改动                                                                                                                                              |
    68	| ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
    69	| `packages/mdx-bridge`          | 删 `package.json#dependencies` 的 `@skb/block-foundation` + `@skb/content-types`；`tsconfig.json` 已无相关 references，无需改；`CONTRACT.md` § "Wave 1 declared peers" 改 prose（描述 forward-compat 意图，不再宣称 deps 占位）。 |
    70	| `packages/block-foundation`    | 删 `package.json#dependencies` 的 `@skb/content-types`；删 `tsconfig.json#references` 中 `{ "path": "../content-types" }`（与 package.json 同步）；`CONTRACT.md` 加一段 prose 说明 Wave 2 引入 content-types 的时机。 |
    71	
    72	`structure-auditor` 月度审计（next: 2026-05）增加机械检查项：扫所有
    73	`packages/*/package.json` 的 `@skb/*` deps，与同包 `src/**/*.{ts,tsx}` 的
    74	`from '@skb/...'` import 比对，发现单边即报 violation。
    75	
    76	### D2：block-foundation 注册 API freeze（spec for first real consumer block-callout）
    77	
    78	在 `packages/block-foundation/RFC.md`（新增）写 walkthrough："block-callout
    79	如何注册 core + ui-default 到 BlockRegistry"，覆盖：
    80	
    81	- `registerCore(core)` + `propsSchema` + `mdxComponent` name + `serialize/parse` hook 形状
    82	- `registerUI(ui)` + `uiId` + `EditorView` / `RenderView` 类型契约
    83	- 错误场景（unknown core / 重复 uiId / propsSchema 非 ZodObject）的 throw 行为
    84	- consumer-side 测试样板（`block-callout/src/__tests__/registry-integration.test.ts` 雏形）
    85	
    86	第一个 `block-*` PR（C1: block-callout/core）必须 reference 此 RFC；后续
    87	`block-*` PR 沿用模式。**RFC.md 不是 CONTRACT.md**：CONTRACT.md 规定 public API
    88	形状（已存在），RFC.md 是 walkthrough（教 consumer 如何用）；二者补充而非重叠。
    89	
    90	D2 的实施在 Task A2（owner: `block-foundation-eng`，blocked_by Task A1=本 ADR commit）。
    91	本 ADR 仅 freeze D2 的**目标**与**承诺**；具体 RFC.md 文本由 Task A2 PR 落地。
    92	
    93	## Consequences
    94	
    95	### 正面
    96	
    97	- **audit invariant 简洁可机械化**：structure-auditor 月度增加单条扫描（package.json `@skb/*` ↔ source import），覆盖 D1 不变量。
    98	- **block-foundation 接口在 block-callout 入门前 freeze**：预防 Wave 1 R-round inflation 同源问题（教训源 ADR-0006 instances #1, #5）。
    99	- **YAGNI 原则在 monorepo 层落地**：dead-dep 不再合法，迫使依赖在真消费时声明。
   100	- **Wave 2 plan-draft 阻塞解锁**：Task A1 commit 后 Track C/D/E/G 全部解锁。
   101	
   102	### 负面
   103	
   104	- **若 Phase 3 npm publish 阶段需 forward-compat package.json 形态**（包发布前要把
   105	  optional peer 显式声明），届时需 ADR-N 重新评估；当前判断该顾虑不充分（私有
   106	  workspace 全程 `private: true`）。
   107	- **block-foundation tsconfig 短期失去 content-types reference**：在 D2/D3
   108	  block-callout 落地后会重建（block-callout 的 tsconfig refs 需要 content-types
   109	  + block-foundation；block-foundation 自己若 Wave 2 中段开始 import content-types
   110	  schema 用于 props 验证，再加 ref）。这是预期 churn，不是退化。
   111	
   112	### Risks (mitigated)
   113	
   114	- **风险**：Wave 2 中段 block-foundation 真消费 content-types 时漏加 dep + ref。
   115	  **缓解**：`pnpm typecheck` + structure-auditor 双门会立刻报缺失（import 找不到 / refs 不一致），与 Wave 1 同类问题处理流程一致。
   116	- **风险**：Option A 让"forward-compat 意图"只能进 CONTRACT prose，未来读者可能漏看。
   117	  **缓解**：每个 block-* PR review chain 已强制读 CONTRACT.md（ADR-0007 D2 row 1 触发），prose 段是 review 必经；review 也会检查 dep 与 import 一致。
   118	
   119	## Implementation
   120	
   121	按 ADR-0006 D8 staging protocol（authority + generated/consumed surface 同 bundle staged）：
   122	
   123	1. **同 bundle commit（本 ADR + 应用同 PR）**：
   124	   - `docs/decisions/ADR-0008-wave-2-entry-policies.md`（本 ADR 自身）

   128	   - `packages/block-foundation/package.json`（删 1 条 dead dep）

   130	   - `packages/block-foundation/CONTRACT.md`（加 prose 说明 Wave 2 真消费时机）

   135	3. **structure-auditor 2026-05 月度审计**：增加 D1 机械扫描项；任何 dead-dep
